## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-12T06:39:10.956Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
Now I have the vanilla semantics:

**CanBeReflected** (Projectile.cs:20216):
- `active && friendly && !hostile && damage > 0`
- type 728 (Fallen Star? no — 728 is... let me check. Actually 728 = Super Star Shooter star? 955 = falling star? Let me check ProjectileID: 9 is Falling Star... Actually 728 is "Star Cannon super"? Let me check) — either 728 or 955 → true
- or aiStyle in {1, 2, 8, 21, 24, 28, 29, 131} → true
- else false

Note: interestingly `reflected` flag is NOT checked in CanBeReflected. But `reflected = true` + `friendly = false` + `hostile = true` means the second time, `friendly && !hostile` fails → so CanBeReflected naturally returns false after reflection. So anti-repeat is via friendly/hostile flip, not explicit reflected check. But Projectile.cs:13768 has `if (!reflected)` — let me check that too.

**ReflectProjectile** (NPC.cs:67036):
- Sound Item150 at proj position
- 3 dust of type 31, velocity *= 0.3
- proj.reflected = true
- proj.hostile = true, proj.friendly = false
- vector = owner player Center - proj.Center, normalized, * oldVelocity.Length()
- velocity = random dir * vector.Length() ... then += vector*20, normalize, * vector.Length() — essentially: direction = normalize(randomDir*len + towardOwner*20), speed = old speed

- owner is NOT changed (stays original player, which is used to compute the "back to owner" direction)
````

```
16	/** 共用绘制：投射物贴图按角度旋转（贴图纵向，rotation = ang+π/2 与 AI_001 一致）。
17	 *  世界坐标绘制(Renderer 实体循环运行在世界变换内,勿再自算屏幕坐标) */
18	function drawProj(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera, e: { x: number; y: number; w: number }, projId: number, ang: number, fallbackColor: string): void {
19	  const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
20	  if (!ctx) return;
21	  const img = projSprite(projId);
22	  ctx.save();
23	  ctx.translate(e.x + e.w / 2, e.y + e.w / 2);
24	  ctx.rotate(ang);
25	  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
26	  if (img && img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
27	    ctx.drawImage(img, -e.w / 2, -e.w / 2, e.w, e.w * (img.naturalHeight / img.naturalWidth));
28	  } else {
29	    ctx.fillStyle = fallbackColor;
30	    ctx.fillRect(-e.w / 2, -e.w / 2, e.w, e.w);
31	  }
32	  ctx.restore();
33	}
34	
35	/** 同敌命中去重 + 冷却的通用伤害结算。
36	 *  hitCd 记录每敌剩余冷却 tick，每 fixedUpdate 调用方先 tickCd() 递减；
37	 *  cooldown >= 9999 视作单次结算（突刺类：一击每敌只中一次） */
38	class HitCooldown {
39	  private map = new Map<number, number>();
40	  tick(): void {
41	    for (const [k, v] of this.map) {
42	      if (v <= 1) this.map.delete(k);
43	      else this.map.set(k, v - 1);
44	    }
45	  }
46	  ready(id: number, cooldown: number): boolean {
47	    if (this.map.has(id)) return false;
48	    if (cooldown < 9999) this.map.set(id, cooldown);
49	    else this.map.set(id, 2147483647); // 永不再中
50	    return true;
51	  }
52	  clear(): void { this.map.clear(); }
53	}
54	
55	function hitEnemies(
56	  self: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; damage: number; knockback: number },
57	  game: GameHooks,
58	  hitCd: HitCooldown,
59	  cooldown: number,
60	  dirX: number,
61	): void {
62	  // 小动物:一击致死(原版小动物是 NPC;近战挥砍同语义)。回旋镖/悠悠球等穿透型
63	  // 命中小动物不消耗穿透语义——hurt 自带 iframes 防同帧连击
64	  hitCritters(self, game);
65	  for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
66	    const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; id: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };
67	    if (e.dead) continue;
68	    if (!(self.x < e.x + e.w && self.x + self.w > e.x && self.y < e.y + e.h && self.y + self.h > e.y)) continue;
69	    if (!hitCd.ready(e.id, cooldown)) continue;
70	    const crit = Math.random() < 0.04;
71	    const dmg = crit ? self.damage * 2 : self.damage;
72	    const kdir = Math.sign((e.x + e.w / 2) - (self.x + self.w / 2)) || dirX;
73	    playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
74	    e.hurt(dmg, kdir * self.knockback * 0.65, -2.5, game);
75	    game.addDamageNumber(self.x + self.w / 2, self.y, Math.round(dmg), crit, crit ? '#FFD040' : '#FF7060');
76	    // 配重球（counterWeight 装备时悠悠球命中落配重：YoyoBag 语义近似——
77	    // spawnWeight 回调由 Game 注入（无实体循环依赖）；伤害 = 武器 100%（原版））
78	    const wSpawn = (self as { spawnWeight?: (x: number, y: number, dmg: number) => void }).spawnWeight;
79	    if (wSpawn) wSpawn(e.x + e.w / 2, e.y, Math.round(self.damage));
80	  }
81	}
82

84

88	  knockback: number;

91	  private rot = 0;
92	  /** 飞出段累计；返回段置 true */
93	  private returning = false;
94	  private hitCd = new HitCooldown();
95	  life = 900;
96	  dead = false;
97	
98	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, knockback: number, projId: number) {
99	    super();
100	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
101	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
102	    this.damage = damage;
103	    this.knockback = knockback;
104	    this.projId = projId;
105	  }
106	
107	  draw(r: Renderer, cam: Camera): void {
108	    drawProj(r, cam, this, this.projId, this.rot, '#C0A060');
109	  }
110	
111	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
112	    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：纯位移外推，不跑命中/碰撞（伤害归拥有者客户端结算）
113	    if (this.netPuppet) { this.netPuppetStep(); return; }
114	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
115	    const p = game.player;
116	    this.rot += 0.35;
117	    this.hitCd.tick();
118	    if (!this.returning) {
119	      // 飞出段：速度衰减（原版摩擦），衰减到阈值/撞墙 → 进入返回段
120	      this.vx *= 0.985;
121	      this.vy *= 0.985;
122	      this.x += this.vx;
123	      this.y += this.vy;
124	      const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
125	      const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
126	      if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty) || Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) < 2.2) {
127	        this.returning = true;
128	        this.hitCd.clear(); // 返程可再次命中（原版去而复返二次伤害语义）
129	        // 轻微弹出防嵌入墙内
130	        this.x -= this.vx; this.y -= this.vy;
131	      }
132	    } else {
133	      // 返回段：朝主角加速（原版 AI_003 尾段朝 owner 归位）
134	      const dx = p.cx - (this.x + this.w / 2);
135	      const dy = p.cy - (this.y + this.h / 2);
136	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
137	      this.vx += (dx / d) * 0.9;
138	      this.vy += (dy / d) * 0.9;
139	      const sp = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
140	      if (sp > 12) { this.vx = (this.vx / sp) * 12; this.vy = (this.vy / sp) * 12; }
141	      this.x += this.vx;
142	      this.y += this.vy;
143	      if (d < 16) { this.dead = true; return; } // 回到手中
144	    }
145	    hitEnemies(this, game, this.hitCd, 20, Math.sign(this.vx));
146	  }
147	}

241	      c.moveTo(this.handX, this.handY);
242	      c.lineTo(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
243	      c.stroke();
244	    }
245	    this.rot += 0.25;
246	    drawProj(r, cam, this, this.projId, this.rot, '#E04040');
247	  }
248	
249	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
250	    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：纯位移外推，不跑命中/碰撞（伤害归拥有者客户端结算）
251	    if (this.netPuppet) { this.netPuppetStep(); return; }
252	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
253	    const p = game.player;
254	    this.handX = p.cx;
255	    this.handY = p.cy;
256	    this.hitCd.tick();
257	    if (!this.channel()) {
258	      // 松手/切走：回收到手（原版 channel 结束语义）
259	      const dx = p.cx - (this.x + this.w / 2);
260	      const dy = p.cy - (this.y + this.h / 2);
261	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
262	      this.vx = (dx / d) * 16;
263	      this.vy = (dy / d) * 16;
264	      this.x += this.vx;
265	      this.y += this.vy;
266	      if (d < 16) this.dead = true;
267	      return;
268	    }
269	    // 追光标（原版 AI_099：朝光标位置加速，限线长内）
270	    const t = this.target();
271	    const dx = t.x - (this.x + this.w / 2);
272	    const dy = t.y - (this.y + this.h / 2);
273	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
274	    this.vx += (dx / d) * 0.55;
275	    this.vy += (dy / d) * 0.55 + (this.flail ? 0.12 : 0.02);
276	    const sp = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
277	    const cap = this.flail ? 11 : 13;
278	    if (sp > cap) { this.vx = (this.vx / sp) * cap; this.vy = (this.vy / sp) * cap; }
279	    this.x += this.vx;
280	    this.y += this.vy;
281	    // 线长约束：超出即向手方向拉回
282	    const ox = this.x + this.w / 2 - p.cx;
283	    const oy = this.y + this.h / 2 - p.cy;
284	    const od = Math.hypot(ox, oy);
285	    if (od > this.stringLen) {
286	      const k = this.stringLen / od;
287	      this.x = p.cx + ox * k - this.w / 2;
288	      this.y = p.cy + oy * k - this.h / 2;
289	    }
290	    // 弹墙（原版 yoyo tileCollide=false 穿墙，连枷也穿；不做碰撞）
291	    hitEnemies(this, game, this.hitCd, 15, Math.sign(this.vx));
292	  }
293	}
294	
295	// ================= 手雷/炸弹/炸药（aiStyle 16，AI_016 引信爆炸） =================
296	
297	export class GrenadeProj extends Entity {
298	  w = 14; h = 14;
299	  vx: number;
300	  vy: number;
301	  damage: number;
302	  projId: number;
303	  /** 引信（原版 timeLeft：手雷 180 / 炸弹 150 / 炸药 135） */
304	  fuse: number;
305	  onExplode: (x: number, y: number, damage: number) => void;
306	  private rot = 0;
307	  dead = false;
308	
309	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, fuse: number,
310	    projId: number, onExplode: (x: number, y: number, damage: number) => void) {
311	    super();
312	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
313	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
314	    this.damage = damage;
315	    this.fuse = fuse;
316	    this.projId = projId;
317	    this.onExplode = onExplode;
318	  }
319	
320	  draw(r: Renderer, cam: Camera): void {
321	    drawProj(r, cam, this, this.projId, this.rot, '#408040');
322	  }
323	
324	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
325	    this.rot += Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) * 0.03;
326	    if (--this.fuse <= 0) {
327	      this.dead = true;
328	      this.onExplode(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2, this.damage);
329	      return;
330	    }
331	    // AI_016：重力 + 弹跳（碰墙法向反弹，保部分动能）。
332	    // 碰撞探测用「底边中点」而非中心——中心探测会让 14px 弹体在地面沉降 ~4px 嵌入
333	    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + 0.3, 16);
334	    let nx = this.x + this.vx;
335	    let ny = this.y + this.vy;
336	    const st = game.world.store;
337	    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
338	      const tx = Math.floor(px / TILE);
339	      const ty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
340	      return st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty);
341	    };
342	    if (solidAt(nx + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2) || solidAt(nx + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h - 1)) {
343	      this.vx *= -0.85; nx = this.x;
344	    }
345	    if (solidAt(nx + this.w / 2, ny + this.h - 1)) {
346	      if (this.vy > 1) { this.vy = -this.vy * 0.5; } else { this.vy = 0; }
347	      this.vx *= 0.92;
348	      ny = this.y;
349	    }
350	    this.x = nx;
351	    this.y = ny;
352	    if (!st.inBounds(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))) this.dead = true;
353	  }
354	}
355	
356	// ================= 荧光棒族（aiStyle 14，AI_014 + 碰撞反弹表 Projectile.cs:23482-23500/18261-18333） =================
357	
358	/** 荧光棒物理 1:1：
359	 *  AI_014 主体（cs:23482-23500）：ai0++ >5 钳位；着地（本帧 vy 未变且 vx≠0）滚动摩擦
360	 *  vx×0.97（<0.01 归零）；重力 vy+=0.2；rotation += vx×0.1
361	 *  粘性（proj 53，tileCollide=false，cs:23245-23296）：与实心块重叠 → velocity=(0,-0.2)
362	 *  抵消重力 → 停在原位粘住
363	 *  妖精（proj 870）：首次碰墙全反射 → aiStyle 170（cs:42997-43053）：半径 10 实心排斥
364	 *  场（速度>1 时每 10 tick 重算）+ 速度 ×0.95 衰减、<1 归零
365	 *  碰撞反弹表（cs:18261-18333，碰墙后）：
366	 *    50      X→-0.2×；Y（lastV>1.5）→-0.2×
367	 *    ≥1089   彩虹：X→-0.99×（|Δ|<1 → ±1）；Y→-0.99×（0<lastV<1 → -1）——近似永动
368	 *    其余    473/515：X→-0.5×；Y（lastV>1）→-0.5×
369	 *  寿命：timeLeft×5（=9000，cs:1075）；矿工 473 ×2（=3600）。到期消失无掉落 */
370	export class GlowstickProj extends Entity {
371	  w: number; h: number;
372	  vx: number;
373	  vy: number;
374	  projId: number;
375	  dead = false;
376	  /** 原版 timeLeft（proj 50/53/515/870/1089 = 9000；473 = 3600） */
377	  timeLeft: number;
378	  private ai0 = 0;
379	  private rot = 0;
380	  /** 妖精阶段（870 反弹后） */
381	  private fairy = false;
382	  private fairyT = 0;
383	
384	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, projId: number) {
385	    super();
386	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
387	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
388	    this.projId = projId;
389	    this.w = projId === 473 ? 8 : 6;
390	    this.h = this.w;
391	    this.timeLeft = projId === 473 ? 3600 : 9000;
392	  }
393	
394	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
395	    // 原版投射物绘制尺寸 = 贴图原生尺寸 × scale（碰撞盒 6×6 与绘制解耦）——
396	    // 50/53/515/1089 贴图是 22×6 横条（此前按碰撞盒 6px 画成 1.6px 细丝）；
397	    // 870 妖精 = 18×96 纵条 6 帧（帧进 5t）；473 = 18×18 单帧
398	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
399	    if (!ctx) return;
400	    const img = projSprite(this.projId);
401	    ctx.save();
402	    ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
403	    ctx.rotate(this.rot);
404	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
405	    if (img && img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
406	      if (this.projId === 870) {
407	        const fr = Math.floor(this.fairyT / 5) % 6;
408	        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, fr * 16, 18, 16, -9, -8, 18, 16);
409	      } else {
410	        ctx.drawImage(img, -img.naturalWidth / 2, -img.naturalHeight / 2);
411	      }
412	    } else {
413	      ctx.fillStyle = '#7FE8A8';
414	      ctx.fillRect(-3, -3, 6, 6);
415	    }
416	    ctx.restore();
417	  }
418	
419	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
420	    if (--this.timeLeft <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
421	    const st = game.world.store;
422	    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
423	      const tx = Math.floor(px / TILE), ty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
424	      return st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty);
425	    };
426	    if (this.fairy) {
427	      // AI_170_FairyGlowstick（cs:42997-43053）：排斥场 + 衰减
428	      this.fairyT++;
429	      if (this.fairyT % 10 === 0 && Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 1) {
430	        const ptx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE), pty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
431	        let zx = 0, zy = 0, n = 0;
432	        for (let i = ptx - 10; i <= ptx + 10; i++) {
433	          for (let j = pty - 10; j <= pty + 10; j++) {
434	            if (!st.inBounds(i, j) || !st.isSolid(i, j)) continue;
435	            const dx = ptx - i, dy = pty - j;
436	            const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
437	            const f = Math.max(10 - len, 0) / len * 3;
438	            zx += dx * f; zy += dy * f; n++;
439	          }
440	        }
441	        if (n > 0) { zx /= n; zy /= n; }
442	        if (Math.hypot(zx, zy) > 1) { this.vx = zx; this.vy = zy; }
443	      }
444	      this.vx *= 0.95; this.vy *= 0.95;
445	      if (Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) < 1) { this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0; }
446	      this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
447	      return;
448	    }
449	    // AI_014 主体
450	    this.ai0++;
451	    if (this.ai0 > 5) {
452	      this.ai0 = 5;
453	      // 着地判定用上一帧 vy 是否未变（原版 velocity.Y == 0 即已落地）——落地格 vy 被置 0
454	      if (this.vy === 0 && this.vx !== 0) {
455	        this.vx *= 0.97;
456	        if (Math.abs(this.vx) < 0.01) this.vx = 0;
457	      }
458	      this.vy += 0.2;
459	    }
460	    this.rot += this.vx * 0.1;
461	    // 粘性（53）：tileCollide=false，与实心重叠 → 粘住
462	    if (this.projId === 53) {
463	      const overlap = solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2)
464	        || solidAt(this.x + 1, this.y + 1) || solidAt(this.x + this.w - 2, this.y + this.h - 2);
465	      if (overlap) { this.vx = 0; this.vy = -0.2; return; }
466	      this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
467	      return;
468	    }
469	    // 移动 + 碰撞反弹（反弹表 cs:18261-18333）
470	    const lastVx = this.vx, lastVy = this.vy;
471	    let nx = this.x + this.vx, ny = this.y + this.vy;
472	    let hitX = false, hitY = false;
473	    if (solidAt(nx + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2) || solidAt(nx + this.w / 2, this.y + 1)) { hitX = true; nx = this.x; }
474	    if (solidAt(nx + this.w / 2, ny + this.h) || solidAt(nx + 1, ny + this.h) || solidAt(nx + this.w - 1, ny + this.h)) { hitY = true; ny = this.y; }
475	    if (hitX || hitY) {
476	      if (this.projId === 870) {
477	        // 妖精首次反弹：全反射进入 170 阶段（cs:18270-18276）
478	        this.vx = -lastVx; this.vy = -lastVy;
479	        this.fairy = true;
480	        this.fairyT = 0;
481	        this.rot = 0;
482	        return;
483	      }
484	      if (this.projId === 50) {
485	        if (hitX) this.vx = lastVx * -0.2;
486	        if (hitY) this.vy = lastVy > 1.5 ? lastVy * -0.2 : 0;
487	      } else if (this.projId >= 1089) {
488	        // 彩虹：近全反射、最低 ±1 保底（cs:18279-18299）
489	        if (hitX) this.vx = Math.abs(this.vx - lastVx) < 1 ? (lastVx < 0 ? -1 : 1) : -lastVx * 0.99;
490	        if (hitY) this.vy = -lastVy * 0.99;
491	      } else {
492	        // 473 矿工 / 515 弹力（默认档，cs:18314-18327）
493	        if (hitX) this.vx = lastVx * -0.5;
494	        if (hitY) this.vy = lastVy > 1 ? lastVy * -0.5 : 0;
495	      }
496	    }
497	    this.x = nx; this.y = ny;
498	  }
499	}
500	
501	// ================= Torch God 火把弹幕（proj 949，AI_184 cs:40100） =================
502	
503	/** 直线飞行火把：射程(rangePx)耗尽前穿墙，之后撞块即毁；命中玩家 20 伤。
504	 *  ai0=尘色/ai1=射程——尘迹省略（粒子钩子未接），伤害/碰撞 1:1 */
505	export class TorchGodProj extends Entity {
506	  w = 4; h = 4;
507	  vx: number; vy: number;
508	  damage = 20;
509	  projId = 949;
510	  dead = false;
511	  private rangeLeft: number;
512	  private tileCollide = false;
513	
514	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, rangePx: number) {
515	    super();
516	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
517	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
518	    this.rangeLeft = rangePx;
519	  }
520	
521	  draw(r: Renderer, cam: Camera): void {
522	    drawProj(r, cam, this, 949, Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI / 2, '#FFB060');
523	  }
524	
525	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
526	    const st = game.world.store;
527	    // ai[1] -= velocity.Length()（:40112-40119）：射程内 tileCollide=false
528	    this.rangeLeft -= Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
529	    if (this.rangeLeft <= 0) this.tileCollide = true;
530	    const nx = this.x + this.vx, ny = this.y + this.vy;
531	    if (this.tileCollide) {
532	      const tx = Math.floor((nx + this.w / 2) / TILE), ty = Math.floor((ny + this.h / 2) / TILE);
533	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { this.dead = true; return; }
534	    }
535	    // 命中玩家（无敌帧由 Player.damage 侧处理）
536	    const p = game.player;
537	    if (nx < p.x + p.w && nx + this.w > p.x && ny < p.y + p.h && ny + this.h > p.y) {
538	      p.damage(this.damage, Math.sign(p.cx - (nx + this.w / 2)) || 1, 0, game as never);
539	      this.dead = true;
540	      return;
541	    }
542	    this.x = nx; this.y = ny;
543	  }
544	}
545
```

```
---
// 弹幕命中目标:怪物桶之外还有小动物桶(critters)。
// 原版小动物是 NPC(NPCID.Squirrel 等),任何伤害即死——近战挥砍已覆盖
// (Game 挥砍段"小动物:任何挥击一击致死"),弹幕此前漏判,箭射松鼠无效果。
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import { BuffType } from '../stats/Buffs';

export interface CritterLike {
  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;
  dead: boolean;
  /** 小动物受击:一击致死,返回是否生效(iframes 内 false) */
  hurt(game: GameHooks): boolean;
}

/** 弹幕与实体 AABB 重叠 */
export function projOverlap(ax: number, ay: number, aw: number, ah: number, e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }): boolean {
  return ax < e.x + e.w && ax + aw > e.x && ay < e.y + e.h && ay + ah > e.y;
}

/** 命中小动物:一击致死。返回是否命中(调用方按各自语义消耗穿透/销毁弹体) */
export function hitCritters(self: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }, game: GameHooks): boolean {
  const list = (game as unknown as { critters?: () => unknown[] }).critters?.() ?? [];
  for (const ent of list) {
    const c = ent as CritterLike;
    if (c.dead) continue;
    if (projOverlap(self.x, self.y, self.w, self.h, c)) {
      c.hurt(game);
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/** 弹幕命中怪物:播受击音效(原版 NPC.StrikeNPC 语义——任意伤害来源都响,
 *  近战挥砍早已如此;弹幕此前漏了) */
export function playEnemyHitSound(game: GameHooks, e: { def?: { hitSound?: string[] } }): void {
  const files = e.def?.hitSound;
  if (files && files.length) game.playSfxFiles(files, 0.9);
}

/** 敌对弹幕命中玩家(原版 Projectile.Damage_EVP,Projectile.cs:13706-13830):
 *  - 门禁 hostile + damage>0(:13708)——调用方用 hostile 标志控制,本函数只做判定;
 *  - 玩家 dead / 无敌即跳过(:13732 player.immune)——Player.damage 自带 iframes,
 *    与原版 immune 同义去重,无需调用方另记冷却;
 *  - 相交判定 = AABB 重叠(Colliding :13749,Rect.Intersects 同构);
 *  - 伤害结算走 GameHooks.damagePlayer(= Player.Hurt 入口,含防御减算/击退/
 *    受击音/伤害数字/饰品触发)。
 *  原版敌弹命中【不消耗穿透】(:13811-13829 仅 435/436/437/681/682 特判递减),
 *  弹体照常飞行——调用方不得据此销毁弹体。
 *  返回是否【实际造成伤害】(iframes/闪避吞掉时 false,对应原版 Hurt 返回 0;
 *  StatusPlayer 的 debuff 授予以此为准,:13798-13800)。 */
export function hitPlayer(
  self: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number },
  game: GameHooks,
  dmg: number,
): boolean {
  if (dmg <= 0) return false;
  const p = game.player;
  if (p.dead) return false;
  if (p.iframes > 0) return false; // 原版 :13732 player.immune 门禁（General 槽）
  if (!projOverlap(self.x, self.y, self.w, self.h, p)) return false;
  const ifr = p.iframes;
  game.damagePlayer(dmg, self.x + self.w / 2, self.y + self.h / 2);
  return p.iframes > ifr; // damage 落地才置无敌帧（闪避不置，对应 Hurt 返回 0）
}

/** 敌弹命中玩家的状态授予(原版 Projectile.StatusPlayer :11450+,仅在
 *  hitPlayer 返回 true 即 Hurt>0 后调用)。本仓库已实装弹型登记:
 *   348 FrostWave —— Chilled(46) 300/600t(:11554-11557) + Frozen(47)
 *                    概率 20/40/60t 三级(:11558-11567)
 *   349 FrostShard —— Chilled(46) 1/3→600t、否则 1/2→300t(:11580-11588)
 *  其余弹型的 debuff 链(毒/诅咒/灵液/暗影焰等)随对应批次逐条接入。 */
export function statusPlayer(game: GameHooks, projId: number): void {
  const p = game.player;
  switch (projId) {
    case 348:
      p.buffs.apply(BuffType.Chilled, (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 300 : 600) / 60);
      if (Math.random() < 2 / 3) {
        const r = Math.random();
        const t = r < 1 / 16 ? 60 : r < 1 / 12 ? 40 : r < 1 / 8 ? 20 : 0;
        if (t > 0) p.buffs.apply(BuffType.Frozen, t / 60);
```

```
  /** 419 日耀旋刃怪冲刺（NPC.cs:57114-57173，AI_003_Fighters 内 type==419 专属段，
   *  位于通用行走段之前——各分支 return 跳过行走/跳跃/远程，仅留全局重力+碰撞）。
   *  ai[2] 状态机（num40=6 / num41=10 / num42=16）：
   *    >0   落地恢复倒数（触地或受击置 60）
   *    0    巡航：面朝玩家 + 视线 → -1（本 tick 仍走通用段，cs 无 return）
   *    -1..-6  蓄力：vx×0.9
   *    -7   发射：朝 player.Top+(0,-30) 方向 ×16
   *    <-7  冲刺：-17 起 vy+0.15 钳 24（前 10t 无重力增量）；反射弹幕 + 受击 ×3
   *  拖尾渲染（ai2≤-9 oldPos 拖尾）已就位：Renderer NPC_GLOW[419] mode 'trail'。
   *  @returns true = 冲刺族本 tick 已自管重力+碰撞，调用方应跳过通用战士段 */
  private solenianDashAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player): boolean {
    const WINDUP = 6;    // num40：蓄力 tick 数（-1..-6）
    const DASH_SPEED = 16; // num42：冲刺初速
    this.reflectsProjectiles = false;
    this.takenDamageMultiplier = 1;
    if (this.ai2 > 0) this.ai2 -= 1;
    if (this.ai2 === 0) {
      // 巡航待发（cs:57124-57131）：面朝玩家（direction 与玩家水平侧一致）+ 中心视线
      if (((player.cx < this.cx && this.facing < 0) || (player.cx > this.cx && this.facing > 0))
        && this.canSeePlayer(game.world.store, player)) {
        this.ai2 = -1;
      }
      return false;   // 未触发/刚起手 → 本 tick 走通用行走段（cs 该分支无 return）
    }
    if (this.ai2 < 0 && this.ai2 > -WINDUP) {
      // 蓄力减速（cs:57135-57140）
      this.ai2 -= 1;
      this.vx *= 0.9;
      this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
      moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
      return true;
    }
    if (this.ai2 === -WINDUP) {
      // 发射（cs:57141-57152）：朝玩家头顶上方 30px 直冲；零向量退化 (spriteDirection,-1)
      this.ai2 -= 1;
      let dx = player.cx - this.cx;
      let dy = (player.y - 30) - this.cy;   // player.Top + (0,-30)
      let d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (d < 0.001) { dx = this.facing || 1; dy = -1; d = Math.hypot(dx, dy); }
      this.vx = (dx / d) * DASH_SPEED;
      this.vy = (dy / d) * DASH_SPEED;
      this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
      moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
      return true;
    }
    // 冲刺（ai2 < -6，cs:57153-57172）
    this.ai2 -= 1;
    // 落地判定读上一 tick 碰撞结果（原版 velocity.Y==0f 是碰撞后清零值）
    if (this.vy === 0) this.ai2 = 60;
    else if (this.ai2 < -WINDUP - 10) this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + 0.15, 24); // num41=10
    this.reflectsProjectiles = true;
    this.takenDamageMultiplier = 3;
    if (this.justHit) this.ai2 = 60;
    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
    return true;
  }
```

```
37	  const img = projSprite(projId);

39	  const frames = projFrameCount(projId);
40	  const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(frames - 1, frameIdx));
41	  const fh = img.naturalHeight / frames;
42	  if (!Number.isFinite(fh) || fh < 1) return null;
43	  const key = `${projId}|${idx}`;
44	  let c = frameCache.get(key);
45	  if (c) return c;
46	  c = document.createElement('canvas');
47	  c.width = img.naturalWidth;
48	  c.height = Math.round(fh);
49	  const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
50	  cx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
51	  cx.drawImage(img, 0, Math.round(idx * fh), img.naturalWidth, Math.round(fh), 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
52	  if (frameCache.size > 2048) frameCache.clear();
53	  frameCache.set(key, c);
54	  return c;
55	}

203	  private bounceOff(game: GameHooks): boolean {

205	    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
206	      const t0 = Math.floor(px / TILE), t1 = Math.floor(py / TILE);
207	      return st.inBounds(t0, t1) && st.isSolid(t0, t1);
208	    };
209	    let bounced = false;
210	    // X 轴：回退一步脱离实心 → 穿透轴是 X，反弹并退回
211	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2 - this.vx, this.y + this.h / 2)) {
212	      this.x -= this.vx;
213	      this.vx *= -0.5;
214	      bounced = true;
215	    }
216	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - this.vy)) {
217	      this.y -= this.vy;
218	      this.vy = this.vy > 1 ? -this.vy * 0.5 : 0;
219	      bounced = true;
220	    }
221	    return bounced;
222	  }
```

```
// 原版投射物数据（tools/extract-projectiles.mjs 从 Terarria1456 Projectile.cs SetDefaults 提取）。
// 注意 1456 的 SetDefaults 是 if/else-if 链（非 switch），且长矛/悠悠球/连枷等族走
// DefaultTo*() 辅助方法（aiStyle 19/99/15）、悠悠球批量定义在 `type >= A && type <= B` 区间块。
import projJson from './vanilla-projectiles.json';

export interface ProjectileData {
  aiStyle?: number;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  penetrate?: number;
  timeLeft?: number;
  damage?: number;
  light?: number;
  friendly?: boolean;
  melee?: boolean;
  ranged?: boolean;
  magic?: boolean;
  minion?: boolean;
  hostile?: boolean;
  tileCollide?: boolean;
  ignoreWater?: boolean;
}

const TABLE = projJson as unknown as Record<string, ProjectileData>;

/** 原版 projectile type → 投射物数据（无条目返回 null） */
export function projectileData(type: number): ProjectileData | null {
  return TABLE[String(type)] ?? null;
}

// ---- aiStyle 家族常量（ProjectileAIStyleIDs / 源码辅助方法实测值） ----
export const AI_ARROW = 1;        // 直飞弹（箭/子弹/大部分魔法弹）
export const AI_THROWN = 2;       // 抛物线投掷物（手里剑/飞刀，重力 0.3/tick）
export const AI_BOOMERANG = 3;    // 回旋镖（飞出后返回）
export const AI_FLAIL = 15;       // 连枷（抛掷甩链）
export const AI_GRENADE = 16;     // 弹跳爆炸物（手雷/炸弹/炸药，引信爆炸）
export const AI_SPEAR = 19;       // 长矛（随角色前刺）
export const AI_YOYO = 99;        // 悠悠球（按住持续滞留）
export const AI_SHORTSWORD = 161; // 短剑直刺
export const AI_WHIP = 165;       // 鞭
export const AI_GLOWSTICK = 14;   // 荧光棒族（弹跳物理/粘性/妖精）
```

```
225	    }
226	    if (--this.life <= 0) {
227	      // 炮弹族 timeLeft 耗尽同样走 Kill 爆炸（Projectile.Kill 由 timeLeft 归零触发）
228	      if (TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind].blast) this.blastKill(game);
229	      else this.dead = true;
230	      return;
231	    }
232	    const st = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind];
233	    this.age++;
234	    // 首帧发射音（Projectile.AI：162=Projectile.cs:21542-21546 / 281=26830-26833 SoundID.Item14）
235	    if (this.age === 1 && st.launchSfx) game.playSfx(st.launchSfx, 1, this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
236	    // 滚转：尖刺球 aiStyle14 = vx*0.14(1405 :21630)/巨石近似 0.03 | 炮弹族按原版
237	    if (st.spin === true) this.rot += this.vx * (st.spinK ?? 0.03);
238	    else if (st.spin === 'roll') {
239	      // aiStyle2（Projectile.cs:21528）：rotation += (|vx|+|vy|)*0.03*direction
240	      this.rot += (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy)) * 0.03 * (this.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1);
241	    } else if (st.spin === 'bunny') {
242	      // aiStyle49（Projectile.cs:26841）：rotation += vx*0.05 + direction*0.05
243	      this.rot += this.vx * 0.05 + (this.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1) * 0.05;
244	    }
245	    if (this.kind === 'spiky') {
246	      // aiStyle 14 默认尾部(1405 :21600-21608 / 1456 同):vy==0 贴地时额外 vx*=0.95;
247	      // 每帧 vx*=0.98、重力 0.3、终端速度 15.9
248	      if (this.vy === 0) this.vx *= 0.95;
249	      this.vx *= 0.98;
250	      this.vy += 0.3;
251	      if (this.vy > 15.9) this.vy = 15.9;
252	      // 分轴积分 + type 185 反弹响应(1405 :13302-13307 / 1456 :18289-18297,
253	      // 原 TileCollision 把被挡轴速度清零、本段按 lastVelocity 以 0.9 系数弹回):
254	      // X 阻挡 → vx=-lastVx*0.9;Y 阻挡且 lastVy>1 → vy=-lastVy*0.9,否则 vy=0 落定不微弹

```

````
```ts
export interface ReflectTarget {
  reflectsProjectiles?: boolean;
}
/** 弹幕命中敌怪分支的反射门禁 + 执行（原版 Projectile.cs:12050-12060 + NPC.cs:67036-67059）
 *  返回 true = 已反射（调用方应跳过本次伤害结算） */
export function tryReflectProjectile(
  proj: { x,y,w,h,vx,vy, damage, hostile?: boolean, dead?: boolean, reflected?: boolean, ... },
  projId: number,
  e: { reflectsProjectiles?: boolean },
  game: GameHooks,
): boolean {
  if (!e.reflectsProjectiles) return false;
  if (proj.reflected) return false; // 防重复(原版由 friendly/hostile 翻转天然拦截,本仓显式双保险)
  if (!projCanBeReflected(projId, proj)) return false;
  reflectProjectile(proj, game);
  return true;
}
```

reflectProjectile:
```ts
export function reflectProjectile(proj: {...}, game: GameHooks): void {
  const cx = proj.x + proj.w/2, cy = proj.y + proj.h/2;
  // SoundID.Item150（素材未入提取库——playSfxFile 未命中静默,回退 tink 金属弹开近似）
  if (!game.playSfxFile?.('Item_150', 1, cx, cy)) game.playSfx('tink', 0.9, cx, cy);
  // 尘 31 ×3 velocity×0.3（NPC.cs:67039-67043）
  game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#D8D8E0', 3, 0.6, { life: 24, damp: 0.9 });
  proj.reflected = true;
  proj.hostile = true;
  // 反射向量（NPC.cs:67047-67055）：随机方向 ×原速 与 朝发射者方向*20 混合后归一 ×原速
  const p = game.player;
  const speed = Math.hypot(proj.vx, proj.vy);
  let ox = p.cx - cx, oy = p.cy - cy;
  const od = Math.hypot(ox, oy) || 1;
  ox /= od; oy /= od;
  const rx = Math.random() * 2 - 1, ry = Math.random() * 2 - 1;  // rand(-100,101)/norm
  let dx = rx * speed + ox * 20, dy = ry * speed + oy * 20;
  const dl = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
  proj.vx = (dx / dl) * speed;
  proj.vy = (dy / dl) * speed;
  if (speed <= 0.01) { proj.vx = ox * 6; proj.vy = oy * 6; }  // 零速退化:朝玩家方向保底(原版 Normalize 零向量守护)
  // 伤害衰减:damage/=2 两次(整数语义,≈÷4) + penetrate=1(NPC.cs:67056-67058)
  proj.damage = Math.floor(Math.floor(proj.damage / 2) / 2);
}
```

Note: vanilla random dir uses Main.rand.Next(-100,101) both axes then Normalize — uniform-ish on square; my rx,ry in [-1,1) approximates. Good; note in comment.

y: number; w: number; h: number;\n  vx: number; vy: number;\n  damage: number;\n  hostile?: boolean;\n  /** 已反射标记（本仓显式防重复；原版靠 friendly→hostile 翻转天然拦截二次反射） */\n  reflected?: boolean;\n}\n\n/** 原版 Projectile.CanBeReflected（Projectile.cs:20216-20230）：\n *  active && friendly && !hostile && damage>0，且 type∈{728,955} 或\n *  aiStyle∈{1,2,8,21,24,28,29,131}。本仓 friendly 无字段——非 hostile 即友方。 */\nexport function projCanBeReflected(\n  projId: number,\n  proj: Pick<ReflectableProj, 'hostile' | 'damage'>,\n): boolean {\n  if (proj.hostile || !(proj.damage > 0)) return false;\n  if (projId === 728 || projId === 955) return true;\n  return REFLECTABLE_AI_STYLES.has(projectileData(projId)?.aiStyle ?? -1);\n}\n\n/** 原版 NPC.ReflectProjectile（NPC.cs:67036-67059）：\n *  - 音效 Item150 + 尘 31×3（velocity×0.3）；\n *  - reflected=true、friendly=false、hostile=true（owner 不变——\"朝发射者\"\n *    方向读 Main.player[owner]，本仓单主角恒为 game.player）；\n *  - 速度 = normalize(随机方向×原速 + 朝发射者×20) × 原速（:67047-67055，\n *    即随机散射偏向回打玩家；原速取 oldVelocity.Length，此处用当前速度近似）；\n *  - damage ÷2 两次（int 语义 ≈ ÷4）+ penetrate=1（穿透归一，穿透字段的\n *    清理由调用方按各自存储执行）。 */\nexport function reflectProjectile(proj: ReflectableProj, game: GameHooks): void {\n  const cx = proj.x + proj.w / 2, cy = proj.y + proj.h / 2;\n  // Item_150 未入音效提取库（public/sounds 无此 wav）：playSfxFile 未命中静默，\n  // 回退 tink（Tink_0/1 金属弹开近似）——素材补齐后自动切真声\n  if (!game.playSfxFile?.('Item_150', 1, cx, cy)) game.playSfx('tink', 0.9, cx, cy);\n  game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#D8D8E0', 3, 0.6, { life: 24, damp: 0.9 });\n  proj.reflected = true;\n  proj.hostile = true;\n  // 反射向量（NPC.cs:67047-67055）\n  const p = game.player;\n  const speed = Math.hypot(proj.vx, proj.vy);\n  let ox = p.cx - cx, oy = p.cy - cy;\n  const od = Math.hypot(ox, oy);\n  if (od > 0.001) { ox /= od; proj.vy = (dy / dl) * speed; }\n  else { proj.vx = ox * 6; proj.vy = oy * 6; } // 零速退化:低速直飞回发射者\n  // 伤害衰减（int 连除两次）+ penetrate=1（:67056-67058）\n  proj.damage = Math.floor(Math.floor(proj.damage / 2) / 2);\n}\n\n/** 弹体命中敌怪分支的反射门禁 + 执行（原版 Projectile.cs:12050-12060：\n *  flag6 = targetNPC.reflectsProjectiles（getGoodWorld 星弹特例未接入——运行时\n *  无 FTW 标志），命中重叠本身即 NPC.CanReflectProjectile 的 Hitbox 相交）。\n *  返回 true = 已反射，调用方跳过本次伤害结算并结束本帧命中迭代\n *  （对应原版 keepIterating=false; return）。 */\nexport function tryReflectProjectile(\n  proj: ReflectableProj,\n  projId: number,\n  e: { reflectsProjectiles?: boolean },\n  game: GameHooks,\n): boolean {\n  if (!e.reflectsProjectiles) return false;\n  if (proj.reflected) return false; // 显式防重复（原版由 hostile 翻转天然拦截）\n  if (!projCanBeReflected(projId, proj)) return false;\n  reflectProjectile(proj, game);\n  return true;\n}\n\n/** 敌弹命中玩家的状态授予(原版 Projectile.StatusPlayer :11450+,仅在"
}
````

```json
 "new_string": "    // 命中敌人（原版 penetrate：箭 1 射中即停；手里剑 4 穿 4 敌，同敌免疫防连击）\n    // 反射后的弹体 friendly 已翻（原版 Damage NPC 需 friendly 门禁）——不再伤敌怪\n    if (this.reflected) return;\n    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {\n      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; id: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] }; reflectsProjectiles?: boolean };\n      if (e.dead) continue;\n      if (this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y) {\n        // 反射源（419 旋刃冲刺等 reflectsProjectiles 置标）：命中前先走反射\n        // （原版 Projectile.cs:12050-12060，反射即终止本帧命中迭代 keepIterating=false）\n        if (tryReflectProjectile(this, this.projId, e, game)) {\n          this.pierce = 1;            // 原版 penetrate=1（NPC.cs:67058）\n          this.hitSet.clear();        // 转敌对后命中表作废\n          return;\n        }\n        if (this.hitSet.has(e.id)) continue; // 穿透弹已命中过此敌"
}
```

```
For the velocity magnitude: speed preserved = hypot(8,0)=8 (grav 0).

Careful: reflected direction is random-blended; with speed 8: dx = rx*8 + ox*20. If rx = -1 (worst case), dx = -8 + 20*ox where ox≈ +0.99 (pointing from enemy to player... wait ox = p.cx - cx negative if player left. Let me define: player at left → ox ≈ -1. dx = rx*8 + (-20). rx∈[-1,1) → dx ∈ [-28, -12) always negative. dy = ry*8 + oy*20, oy≈0 → dy ∈ [-8,8). After normalize, vx = dx/dl*8 → vx negative guaranteed. 

But if speed is small relative to 20 (e.g. speed 2), dx = rx*2 - 20 → still dominated by ox*20. Good, direction robust. Note: the reflected arrow then flies toward player and with grav 0 it'll hit the player → damagePlayer called with damage 10. In test, use grav 0 and few steps to avoid hitting player, or place player far. Player at 100, enemy at 320 — reflected arrow travels left, will reach player after ~25 ticks. Keep steps small.

Also the arrow after reflection: hostile=true → `if (this.hostile && hitPlayer(...)) statusPlayer(...)`. Fine.

Test "二次命中不重复反射": after reflection, step arrow back and forth? Simplest: directly call tryReflectProjectile again → returns false; and arrow.reflected gate in loop skips. Probe: reposition arrow onto the enemy again and step → e.hurt not called, reflect stays once (check projCanBeReflected(this.projId, arrow) === false because hostile).

Test TrapShot: cannonball TrapShot(x,y,vx,vy,dmg,'cannonball'), enemy overlap, friendly → reflect → hostile=true. And 'spiky' (hostile) → no reflect. But TrapShot fixedUpdate does collision/age stuff — stepping may kill it on tiles? World air; spiky has gravity, falls; fine for a few steps. TrapShot damage: hostile gate — cannonball friendly (hostile=false). Note damage>0.

Also check TrapShot blast: cannonball has blast → on wall explode; air world fine.

Enemy hurt spy for TrapShot: same.

For netPuppet check: Arrow has netPuppet field from Entity? Check.
```

```
194	        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
195	        e.hurt(dmg, Math.sign(this.vx) * this.knockback, 0, game);
196	        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, Math.round(dmg), crit, crit ? '#FF8040' : '#FFD060');
```
